10
Conclusion

In this work, we have proposed and analyzed a second-order numerical scheme based on MUSCL-type reconstruction for conservation laws with discontinuous flux functions, where the flux may exhibit multiple discontinuities. The scheme features a simple formulation inspired by the Nessyahu-Tadmor central scheme. We employ the framework of compensated compactness to establish the convergence of the proposed scheme. This analysis requires several key estimates, which we carefully derive by exploiting the structural properties of the numerical method.

The uniqueness of solutions to conservation laws with discontinuous flux is a topic of considerable interest, and various approaches exist in the literature. In this work, we focus on a Kruzkov-type entropy condition equivalent to the vanishing viscosity solution approach. We establish the convergence of entropy solutions by incorporating a mesh-dependent parameter into the scheme. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive convergence result, including entropy solutions, for a MUSCL-type reconstruction scheme applied to conservation laws with discontinuous flux.
The numerical solutions presented in Section
9
, where we compare the proposed second-order scheme and the first-order Lax-Friedrichs (LF) scheme, clearly indicate the superiority of the SO scheme over its FO counterpart.

This work opens up several avenues for further research. For instance, investigating the convergence of the MUSCL-Hancock
[
61
]
scheme and Runge-Kutta MUSCL
[
31
]
schemes, as well as the convergence of schemes using Godunov-type numerical interface fluxes. Additionally, it would be interesting to explore convergence to the (A, B)-entropy solution for arbitrary admissible (A, B)-connections. Extending the convergence analysis of MUSCL-type schemes to multidimensional cases is also a compelling area for future research. Furthermore, we note that
the convergence analysis presented in this work relies on certain a priori estimates (Lemmas
9
,
10
,
11
), which require the flux function to be strictly convex (or strictly concave). Extending this analysis to encompass more general flux functions remains an open question for future research. We aim to address these topics in forthcoming papers.

Appendix A
Proof of Lemma
9

The corresponding estimate for the case when
k\equiv 1,
was derived in
[
52
]
. We follow a similar approach for the problem (
1.1
), where the coefficient
k
can be discontinuous.

A.1
A bound on non-decreasing sequences

In this section,
we establish a result regarding the jumps generated by applying the scheme (
3.4
) to non-decreasing sequences with finitely many non-zero jumps. The main result is stated in the following lemma, and its proof is provided at the end of this section.

Lemma A.20
.

Consider a non-decreasing sequence
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
such that for some
l,r\in\mathbb{Z},

the jumps
\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}:=u_{j+1}-u_{j}=0
for all
j\notin\{l-1,l,\dots,r-1\}.
Further, let
\lvert u_{j}\rvert\leq\lVert C_{u_{0}}\rVert,\,\forall{j\in\mathbb{Z}},
where
C_{u_{0}}
is as in (
5.3
).
Let
\{u^{\prime}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
be obtained from
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
by applying the time-update formula (
3.4
) and
denote the corresponding jumps
\delta_{j}^{\prime}:=u_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{\prime}-u_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\prime}
for
{j\in\mathbb{Z}}
. Under the CFL condition (
6.1
) there exists a constant
\Theta\geq 0
independent of
\Delta x
such that
the jump sequence
\{\delta_{j}^{\prime}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
satisfies the following estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{500}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+\frac{1}{6400}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

(A.1)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\Theta\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+2}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert,

where
\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}:=\Delta\delta_{j-1}-\Delta\delta_{j-2}.

To prove Lemma
A.20
, we need to establish several auxiliary lemmas, starting with the following one.

Lemma A.21
.

Let
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
and
\{\delta^{\prime}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
be as in Lemma
A.20
. Under the CFL condition (
6.1
)
the jumps
\{\delta_{j}^{\prime}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
satisfy the following estimate

\displaystyle\lvert\delta_{j}^{\prime}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right).

(A.2)

Proof A.22
.

From the scheme (
3.4
), the jump
\delta_{j}^{\prime}
can be written as

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\lambda\left(f(k_{j+1},u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}})-2f(k_{j},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})+f(k_{j-1},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)

(A.3)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}(s_{j+1}-2s_{j}+s_{j-1}),

where we define
u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}:=u_{j}-\frac{\lambda}{2}f_{u}(k_{j},u_{j})s_{j},

s_{j}:=\min\{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}},\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\},{j\in\mathbb{Z}}.
We can also write

u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}=\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{\lambda}{2}(a_{j+1}s_{j+1}-a_{j}s_{j}),

where,

\displaystyle a_{j}:=f_{u}(k_{j},u_{j}),\,\mbox{for}\,j\in\mathbb{Z}.

(A.4)

Now, by adding and subtracting the term
f(k_{j},u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}})
and using the mean value theorem, we can write

\displaystyle f(k_{j+1},u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f(k_{j},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle=f_{k}(c_{12},u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}})\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+f_{u}(k_{j},\zeta_{12})(u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle=\bar{b}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{\lambda}{2}(a_{j+1}s_{j+1}-a_{j}s_{j})\right),

where we define

\displaystyle\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle:=f_{u}(k_{j},\zeta),\quad\bar{b}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}:=f_{k}(c,u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

(A.5)

for some
\zeta\in\mathcal{I}(u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}})
and
c\in\mathcal{I}(k_{j},k_{j+1}).

Now, (
A.3
) reduces to

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\frac{1}{8}(\Delta s_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\lambda\left[\bar{b}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right]

(A.6)

\displaystyle-\lambda\left[\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{\lambda}{2}(a_{j+1}s_{j+1}-a_{j}s_{j})\right)\right.

\displaystyle}{}{\displaystyle\left.-\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{\lambda}{2}(a_{j}s_{j}-a_{j-1}s_{j-1})\right)\right],

The CFL condition (
6.1
) implies that
\kappa\leq\frac{1}{4},
which yields the following upper bound

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{8}+\kappa+\kappa^{2}\right)(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right)

(A.7)

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right).

Analogously, we obtain a lower bound

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{8}-\kappa-\kappa^{2}\right)(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right)

(A.8)

\displaystyle\geq-\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right).

The estimates (
A.7
) and (
A.8
) together imply that

\displaystyle\lvert\delta_{j}^{\prime}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right).

(A.9)

This completes the proof.

Lemma A.23
.

Let
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
and
\{\delta^{\prime}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
be as in Lemma
A.20
, with
\lvert u_{j}\rvert\leq C_{u_{0}},
for all
{j\in\mathbb{Z}},
(where
C_{u_{0}}
is as in (
5.3
)). Define
\delta^{\prime\prime}_{j}
by replacing the term
(a_{j+1}^{n}s_{j+1}^{n}-a_{j}^{n}s_{j}^{n})
with
\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}\Delta s_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}
in
\delta_{j}^{\prime}
(
A.6
), where
\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}
is as defined in (
A.5
).
Then

\displaystyle\Big\lvert\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}(\delta_{j}^{\prime})^{2}-\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}(\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\Big\rvert

\displaystyle\leq 55\lambda^{2}\gamma_{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})^{3}+P_{1},

(A.10)

where
P_{1}:=(16\lambda^{2}C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\lVert f_{uk}\rVert+44\lambda^{3}\lVert f_{k}\rVert\gamma_{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}+16\lambda^{3}\lVert f_{k}\rVert C_{u_{0}}\lVert k\rVert)\\
\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert.

Proof A.24
.

The jump term
\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}
is given by

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\frac{1}{8}(\Delta s_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\lambda\left(\bar{b}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

(A.11)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\lambda\left(\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{\lambda}{2}a_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\right)-\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{\lambda}{2}\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right).

With arguments analogous to those used in (
A.7
), it follows that

\displaystyle\lvert\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right).

(A.12)

Now, we write the difference
\delta_{j}^{\prime}-\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}
as

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime}-\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}(\tau_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\tau_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}),

(A.13)

where
\tau_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}:=\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\left(a_{j+1}s_{j+1}-a_{j}s_{j}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\right),
which can be bounded as

\displaystyle\lvert\tau_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\lVert f_{u}\rVert\lvert s_{j+1}(a_{j+1}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})-s_{j}(a_{j}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})\rvert.

(A.14)

Further, adding and subtracting the term
f_{u}(k_{j},u_{j+1})
in
a_{j+1}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}
yields

\displaystyle a_{j+1}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle=f_{uk}(c,u_{j+1})\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+f_{uu}(k_{j},\zeta^{\prime})\left(u_{j+1}-\zeta\right),

(A.15)

where
\zeta
is as in (
A.5
),
c\in\mathcal{I}(k_{j},k_{j+1})
and
\zeta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}(\zeta,u_{{j+1}}).

Now, due to the CFL condition (
6.1
), it follows that

\displaystyle u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=u_{j+1}-\frac{1}{2}\lambda a_{{j+1}}s_{j+1}\leq u_{j+1}+\frac{\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}=:u_{j+1}^{+}\quad\mbox{and}

(A.16)

\displaystyle u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=u_{j+1}-\frac{1}{2}\lambda a_{j+1}s_{j+1}\geq u_{j+1}-\frac{\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}=:u_{j+1}^{-},\quad\mbox{for}\,{j\in\mathbb{Z}}.

The CFL condition (
6.1
) and (
A.16
) ensure that
u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in[u_{j}^{-},u_{j+1}]
and
u_{{j+1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\in[u_{j},u_{j+1}^{+}].
Combining this with the fact that
\zeta\in\mathcal{I}(u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}),
we derive the following estimate:

\displaystyle\lvert u_{j+1}-\zeta\rvert\leq\max\{u_{j+1}-u_{j}^{-},u_{j+1}^{+}-u_{j+1}\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{4},\frac{\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}\right\}

(A.17)

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{4},

which, when combined with (
A.15
) and hypothesis
(H2)
, provides the following upper bound

\displaystyle\lvert a_{j+1}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\leq\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\gamma_{2}\left(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}\right).

(A.18)

In a similar way, we have

\displaystyle\lvert a_{j}-\bar{a}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle=\lvert f_{u}(k_{j},u_{j})-f_{u}(k_{j},\zeta)\rvert=\lvert f_{uu}(k_{j},\bar{\zeta})\left(u_{j}-\zeta\right)\rvert\leq\gamma_{2}\lvert u_{j}-\zeta\rvert,

(A.19)

where
\bar{\zeta}\in\mathcal{I}(\zeta,u_{j}).

Here, analogous to (
A.17
),
\lvert u_{j}-\zeta\rvert
can be bounded as

\displaystyle\lvert u_{j}-\zeta\rvert\leq\max\{u_{j}-u_{j}^{-},u_{j+1}^{+}-u_{j}\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{4},\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}\right\}

(A.20)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5}{4}\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{1}{4}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}.

Using (
A.18
), (
A.19
) and (
A.20
) in (
A.14
), we obtain an estimate on the term
\tau_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}
in (
A.13
):

\displaystyle\lvert\tau_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{9}{4}\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{1}{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)+\lVert f_{u}\rVert\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert.

(A.21)

The estimate (
A.21
) applied on (
A.13
) yields

\displaystyle\lvert\delta_{j}^{\prime}-\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\gamma_{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\left(\frac{9}{4}+\frac{1}{2}+\frac{9}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\right)M_{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}

(A.22)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\left(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right),

where
M_{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}:=\max\left\{(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})^{2},(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2},(\delta_{j-\frac{3}{2}})^{2}\right\}.

Making use of the estimate (
A.22
) together with (
A.9
) and (
A.12
) and using the inequality
ab^{2}\leq a^{3}+b^{3},
for
a,b\geq 0,
it follows that

\displaystyle\Big\lvert\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}(\delta_{j}^{\prime})^{2}-\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}(\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\Big\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}\lvert\delta_{j}^{\prime}-\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}\rvert\lvert\delta_{j}^{\prime}+\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}\rvert

(A.23)

\displaystyle\leq 55\lambda^{2}\gamma_{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+P_{1}.

To obtain
P_{1}
, we have used the bound
\lvert\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\leq 2C_{u_{0}}.
This completes the proof.

Now we define the term
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\right)
and write this in a suitable form to obtain a lower bound. We begin with the notations

\displaystyle\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}:=\frac{1}{2}+\lambda\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\,\,\mbox{and}\,\,\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}:=\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}),

(A.24)

and reformulate the modified jumps (
A.11
) as

\displaystyle\delta_{j-1}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{j-\frac{3}{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

(A.25)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\lambda\left(\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right).

Plugging in the expression (
A.25
), we can write
\mathcal{D}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2}+\mathcal{I}_{3}+P_{2},

(A.26)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{2},

(A.27)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{j-\frac{3}{2}}\right)\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle P_{2}

\displaystyle:=-\lambda^{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+2\lambda\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\lambda\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{b}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right).

Noting that
\lvert\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\leq 1
and
\lvert\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\leq\frac{1}{2},
for
{j\in\mathbb{Z}}
(by the CFL condition (
6.1
)), we obtain

\displaystyle\lvert P_{2}\rvert\leq\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert(8\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\lVert k\rVert+24C_{u_{0}}).

(A.28)

Upon a change of index in the summation and grouping the terms appropriately, the term
\mathcal{I}_{1}
can be rewritten as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\left(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}-(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}\right)-2\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

(A.29)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}+\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.{\hskip 14.22636pt}-2\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{2}+(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\right).

Now, we write

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}s_{j-1}\right)^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{1},

(A.30)

where
\mathcal{E}_{1}:=\mathcal{I}_{3}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}s_{j-1}\right)^{2}.

Incorporating the expressions (
A.29
) and (
A.30
) into (
A.26
) and by adding and subtracting
\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})
the term
\mathcal{D}
is reformulated as

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{Q}_{1}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}+P_{2}+\mathcal{E}_{1},

(A.31)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})(\Delta\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}+\mathcal{I}_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}),

(A.32)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{4}\left(2\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}s_{j-1}\right)^{2}\right)\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}+(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}\right).

Remark A.25
.

It is possible to derive a bound for the term
\mathcal{E}_{1}
in (
A.31
). To show this, we begin by rewriting it as

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\right)

(A.33)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(2\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right).

Immediately, we have the following bound:

\displaystyle\lvert\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle=\lambda^{2}\lvert(\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}\rvert\leq\lambda^{2}\lvert\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert\lvert\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

(A.34)

Next, using the definition of
\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}
from (
A.5
) and adding and subtracting the term
f_{u}(k_{j-2},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}),
we get

\displaystyle\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle=f_{uk}(\bar{k}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+f_{uu}(k_{j-2},\bar{\bar{u}}_{j-1})(\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}),

(A.35)

for some
\bar{k}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{j-2},k_{j-1}),

\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\in\mathcal{I}(u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\in\mathcal{I}(u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})
and
\bar{\bar{u}}_{j-1}\in\mathcal{I}(\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}).

Further, by the CFL restriction (
6.1
) and using arguments similar to those in (
A.16
), we have
\displaystyle\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\in[u_{j-1}-\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}}{4},u_{j}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}]
and
\displaystyle\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\in[u_{j-2}-\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}}{4},u_{j-1}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}}{4}].
This in turn, implies that

\displaystyle\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq u_{j}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{4}-u_{j-2}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}}{4}

\displaystyle=(u_{j}-u_{j-1})+(u_{j-1}-u_{j-2})+\frac{1}{4}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})\leq\frac{5}{4}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}),

\displaystyle\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}}{2},

and hence

\displaystyle\lvert\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert\leq\frac{5}{4}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}),

(A.36)

due to the fact that
\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\geq 0,{j\in\mathbb{Z}}.

The estimates (
A.34
) and (
A.36
) together with the hypothesis
(H2)
imply that

\displaystyle\lvert\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\gamma_{2}\frac{5}{4}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})+2\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

(A.37)

By the CFL condition (
6.1
), we have
\lvert\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\leq\frac{1}{2},{j\in\mathbb{Z}}.
Moreover,
\lvert\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\leq 2\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}},{j\in\mathbb{Z}}
and
ab^{2}\leq a^{3}+b^{3},
for any
a,b\geq 0.
Invoking these observations and the estimate (
A.37
) into the expression (
A.33
), we obtain the desired bound

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{1}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{35}{2}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\gamma_{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+24\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

(A.38)

Lemma A.26
.

Let
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
be as in Lemma
A.20
. Then for
\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}
in (
A.24
),

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}\right)+\lambda\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}f_{ku}(\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}),

(A.39)

for
\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{j-1},k_{j-2}).

Proof A.27
.

Using the fact that
\displaystyle\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}=\frac{f(k_{j-1},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f(k_{j-1},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})}{u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\mbox{for}\,{j\in\mathbb{Z}},
the term
\Delta\alpha_{j-1}=\lambda(\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})
can be written as follows

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}

(A.40)

\displaystyle=\lambda\left(\frac{f(k_{j-2},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f(k_{j-2},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})}{u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{f(k_{j-2},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f(k_{j-2},u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}})}{u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\lambda\left(\frac{\left(f(k_{j-1},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f(k_{j-2},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)-\left(f(k_{j-1},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f(k_{j-2},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)}{u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\lambda\bar{f}_{j-2}\left[u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\left(u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\lambda\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{f_{k}(\bar{k}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}})-f_{k}(\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}})}{u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda}{2}f_{uu}(k_{j-2},\bar{\zeta})\left(u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\lambda\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}f_{ku}(\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},\bar{u}_{j-\frac{1}{2}}),

where
f_{j-2}(\cdot)
denotes the denote the divided difference of the function
f(k_{j-2},\cdot),

\bar{\zeta}\in\left(\min\{u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}\},\max\{u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}\}\right),

\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\in\mathcal{I}(u_{j-1}^{\frac{1}{2}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}),
and
\bar{k}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{j-1},k_{j-2}).
The second term in the last step of (
A.40
) is derived by applying assumption
(H3)
,
f_{kk}=0,
and adding and subtracting
f_{k}(\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Now, the CFL condition (
6.1
) allows us to write

\displaystyle u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=u_{j}^{n}-\frac{\lambda}{2}f_{u}(k_{j},u_{j}^{n})s_{j}^{n}\geq u_{j}^{n}-\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}}{4}\quad\mbox{and}

(A.41)

\displaystyle u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=u_{j-2}^{n}-\frac{\lambda}{2}f_{u}(k_{j-2},u_{j-2}^{n})s_{j-2}^{n}\leq u_{j-2}^{n}+\frac{\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}}{4},

and hence
u_{j}^{\frac{1}{2}}-u_{j-2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq\frac{3}{4}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}\right).
Using this bound in (
A.40
) together with
(H2)
, yields

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}\right)+\lambda\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}f_{ku}(\bar{\bar{k}}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}).

(A.42)

This completes the proof.

Lemma A.28
.

Let
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}

. The term

Proof A.29
.

Applying summation by parts, the term
\mathcal{I}_{2}

) can be expressed as

using which the term

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}(\Delta\alpha_{j}-\Delta\alpha_{j-1})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2},

where

Clearly,

Utilizing this relation and following arguments similar to those in equations (50)-(53) of

]

Recollecting the estimate (

as follows:

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{6}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert)^{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{16}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}.

(A.59)

Lemma A.30
.

Let
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
be as in Lemma
A.20
. The term
\mathcal{Q}_{2}
in (
A.32
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}=\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}+\mathcal{E}_{7},

(A.60)

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}:=\frac{1}{64}\left(2\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}s_{j-1}\right)^{2}\right)
and

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{7}\rvert\leq\frac{1}{8}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}.

(A.61)

Further, for
\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}
as in (
A.28
), we have the lower bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}\geq\frac{1}{2048}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}.

(A.62)

Proof A.31
.

Adding and subtracting
\frac{1}{4}
to
\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},
the identity (
A.60
) is immediate,
where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{7}:=\frac{1}{4}\left(2\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{1}{4}\right)^{2}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{1}{4}\right)^{2}\left(\Delta^{2}s_{j-1}\right)^{2}\right).

(A.63)

Further, invoking Lemma 4 from
[
53
]
, we have

\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}s_{j-1}\right)^{2}\leq 2\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2},

which, when applied to (
A.63
), implies that

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{7}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{4}\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2},

(A.64)

where the last inequality follows from the CFL condition (
6.1
). Furthermore, following the proof of Lemma 4 in
[
53
]
, we obtain the desired lower bound (
A.62
), thus completing the proof.

Towards our objective of getting a lower bound on
\mathcal{D},
we now reformulate the term
R_{1}
from (
A.44
). By applying summation by parts and subsequently adding and subtracting
\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}
to
\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},
we rewrite the second term of (
A.44
) as

\displaystyle\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}(\Delta\alpha_{j}-\Delta\alpha_{j-1})\right)

(A.65)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+\mathcal{E}_{8},

where
\mathcal{E}_{8}:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},
and is bounded as follows

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{8}\rvert\leq 15\lambda^{3}\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{2}\gamma_{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{3}+16\lambda^{3}\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

(A.66)

Now, in view of (
A.65
), (
A.44
) can be written as

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{E}_{8}+\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}

(A.67)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\leq 0}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+\alpha_{j-\frac{5}{2}}}{2}\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\geq 0}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1+\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\alpha_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{2}\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}.

In (
A.67
), adding and subtracting
\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}
to
\alpha_{j-\frac{5}{2}}
in the fourth term and
\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}
to
\alpha_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}
in the fifth term, we write

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}=\mathcal{E}_{8}+\mathcal{R}_{2}+\mathcal{R}_{3}+\mathcal{R}_{4}+\mathcal{R}_{5},

(A.68)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right),

(A.69)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{3}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1-\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\right)\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{4}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\leq 0}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\alpha_{j-\frac{5}{2}}}{2}\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{5}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\geq 0}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{2}\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}.

Next,
\mathcal{R}_{4}
admits a lower bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{4}\geq-16(C_{u_{0}})^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\sum_{j=l-2}^{r}\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{3}{2}}\rvert=:\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{4}.

(A.70)

This holds true because, by (
A.42
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{4}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\leq 0}\frac{\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}}{2}\left(\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}^{n}\right)+\lambda\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}f_{ku}(\bar{k}_{j-\frac{5}{2}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{16}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\leq 0}\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}^{n}\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\leq 0}\lambda\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}f_{ku}(\bar{k}_{j-\frac{5}{2}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\Delta\delta_{j-1}\leq 0}\lambda\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}f_{ku}(\bar{k}_{j-\frac{5}{2}},\bar{u}_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2},

and also by noting the fact that
(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}\leq 16(C_{u_{0}})^{2}.
Similar arguments on
\mathcal{R}_{5}
yield

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{5}\geq-16(C_{u_{0}})^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\sum_{j=l}^{r+2}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert=:\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{5}

(A.71)

Now, using Lemmas
A.28
and
A.30
, along with with the expression (
A.68
) in (
A.31
), we write

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}+\mathcal{R}_{4}+\mathcal{R}_{5}+P_{2}+\mathcal{E}_{9},

(A.72)

where
Q_{3}^{*}:=\mathcal{Q}_{3}+\mathcal{R}_{2}+\mathcal{R}_{3}
and
\mathcal{E}_{9}:=\mathcal{E}_{1}-\mathcal{E}_{4}-\mathcal{E}_{5}-\mathcal{E}_{6}+\mathcal{E}_{7}+\mathcal{E}_{8}.
Further, combining the expressions (
A.38
),(
A.46
), (
A.47
), (
A.59
), (
A.61
) and (
A.66
), using the CFL condition (
6.1
) and after suitable algebraic manipulations, we obtain the following bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{9}

\displaystyle\geq-255\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\gamma_{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}-\frac{21}{16}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

(A.73)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\left(24+192\lVert f_{u}\rVert+16\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{2}\right)C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

Next, we establish a lower bound on the term
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}.

Lemma A.32
.

The term
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}
in (
A.72
) admits the lower bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}\geq\frac{3}{64}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}-6\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\mathcal{E}_{10},

(A.74)

where
C_{u_{0}}
is as in (
5.3
) and
\mathcal{E}_{10}
is bounded as follows

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{10}\rvert\leq\left(28\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\lambda\lVert f_{uk}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\left(\frac{35}{2}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\lambda\gamma_{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}.

Proof A.33
.

Recalling the notation
\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}:=\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})
and using it in
\mathcal{R}_{3}
of (
A.69
), we write
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{3}+\mathcal{R}_{2}-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(1-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}(\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}.

Writing
\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}=\frac{1}{4}+(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{1}{4}),

\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}=\frac{1}{2}+(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{1}{2})\,\mbox{and}\,\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}=\frac{1}{2}+(\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{1}{2}),
the term
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}\right)

(A.75)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\frac{1}{4}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left(\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\frac{1}{4}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{10}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\mathcal{E}_{10},

where

\displaystyle z_{j-1}

\displaystyle:=2(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}+2(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}+\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{10}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\left[\left(\alpha_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n})^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2}-\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})^{2}\right.

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}\left.+\left(\varphi_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{1}{4}\right)\left(\Delta s_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}-\Delta s_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\left(\frac{3}{4}+(\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-2\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)(\Delta\delta_{j-1})^{2}\right].

Recalling the notation
\alpha_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}:=\frac{1}{2}+\lambda\bar{a}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}},
it is clear from (
A.35
) and (
A.36
) that
\Delta\alpha_{j-1}
admits a bound
\lvert\Delta\alpha_{j-1}\rvert\leq\lambda\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\frac{5}{4}\lambda\gamma_{2}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}).
Consequently, we obtain the following bound on
\mathcal{E}_{10}:

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{E}_{10}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{7}{2}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lambda\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}+(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}\right)

(A.76)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\left(\frac{7}{2}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\frac{5}{4}\lambda\gamma_{2}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}+(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{7}{2}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)8\lambda\lVert f_{uk}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\left(\frac{7}{2}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\frac{5}{4}\lambda\gamma_{2}6\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\left(28\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\lambda\lVert f_{uk}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\left(\frac{105}{4}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\lambda\gamma_{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}.

Next, to obtain a lower bound on
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}
, we need a bound on the first term in the RHS of (
A.75
). To this end, we first make the following observation on
\displaystyle z_{j-1}
in (
A.75
):

\displaystyle z_{j-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{2}+s_{j-1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})-\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}s_{j-2}-\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}s_{j}

(A.77)

\displaystyle\geq 2\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+s_{j-1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}).

This establishes that
z_{j-1}\geq 0.

Furthermore, combining the expression (
A.77
) with the
\mathrm{L}^{\infty}
-stability (
5.3
) of the scheme (
3.4
), we deduce that
\lvert z_{j-1}\rvert\leq 24C_{u_{0}}^{2},
for
{j\in\mathbb{Z}}.
Next, we obtain a bound

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}

(A.78)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert(\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert),

which is verified by considering the two possible cases.

Case 1
(
\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}
and
\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\leq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}
): In this case, as
s_{j-2}=\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}},
from the definition of
z_{j-1},
it follows that

\displaystyle z_{j-1}

\displaystyle=(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}+(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}+2\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}+s_{j-1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})-\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}s_{j-2}-\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}s_{j}

\displaystyle\geq(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}-\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}.

This bound, combined with (
A.42
) implies that

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}-\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\right)-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}-(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\right)-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

Furthermore, in this case, we also have

z_{j-2}\geq 2\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},

which yields

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}2\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}^{n}\right)-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4}\lambda\gamma_{1}\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert.

This leads to the conclusion

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert(\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert),

(A.79)

for Case 1.

Case 2
(
\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\geq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}
or
\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\geq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}
): In the case when
\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\geq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}
, we have
s_{j-1}=\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}.
Consequently, applying (
A.77
) yields
z_{j-1}\geq 4(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}.
Further, using (
A.42
), we can write

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}4(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}\right)-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{2}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert.

Moreover, (
A.42
) also implies that
\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}\geq-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert.
Thus,

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert(\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert),

(A.80)

for the case when
\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\geq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}.

Next, for the case when

\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\geq\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}},
we have
s_{j-2}=\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}.
Therefore, using (
A.77
), we have
z_{j-2}\geq 4(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}.

Applying (
A.42
) again, we obtain

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\lambda\gamma_{1}4(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{2}\left(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{j-\frac{5}{2}}^{n}\right)-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{2}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert.

Again, using (
A.77
) it follows that
\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}\geq-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{3}{2}}\rvert,
leading to

\displaystyle\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}})^{3}-24C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert(\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{5}{2}}\rvert),

(A.81)

thus concluding Case 2.

Combining both the cases, the estimates (
A.79
),(
A.80
) and (
A.81
) establish the bound (
A.78
). Furthermore, using (
A.78
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\sum_{j=l}^{r+1}\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\sum_{j=l+1}^{r+2}\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}\right)

(A.82)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\sum_{j=l}^{r+2}\left(\Delta\alpha_{j-1}z_{j-1}+\Delta\alpha_{j-2}z_{j-2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{16}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}-24\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert.

The bound (
A.82
) together with (
A.75
) yields the desired estimate (
A.74
) on
Q_{3}^{*}
.

Finally, to conclude this section on non-decreasing sequences, we provide the proof of Lemma
A.20
.

Proof A.34
(Proof of Lemma
A.20
).

By invoking the bound (
A.62
) for
\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}
from Lemma
A.30
and the bound (
A.74
) for
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}
from Lemma
A.32
, in the expression (
A.72
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{64}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lambda\gamma_{1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+\frac{1}{2048}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{9}+\mathcal{E}_{10}

(A.83)

\displaystyle{}{\displaystyle+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{4}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{5}+P_{2}-6\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert.}

Now, using the bounds (
A.73
) and (
A.76
) for
\mathcal{E}_{9}
and
\mathcal{E}_{10},
respectively, and the CFL condition (
6.1
), we further simplify (
A.83
) as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{64}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+\frac{1}{2048}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

(A.84)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\frac{1125}{4}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\gamma_{2}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}-\frac{21}{16}\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\left(24+220\lVert f_{u}\rVert+16\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{2}\right)C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}{+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{4}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{5}+P_{2}-6\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{15}{1600}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+\frac{1}{6400}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\left(24+220\lVert f_{u}\rVert+16\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{2}\right)C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}{+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{4}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{5}+P_{2}-6\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert},

where the last inequality follows from the CFL condition (
6.1
).
Adding and subtracting
\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}
to
\mathcal{D}
in (
A.84
) and subsequently using (
A.10
) and the CFL condition (
6.1
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\right)-\Big\lvert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\Big\rvert

(A.85)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{500}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+\frac{1}{6400}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}-P_{1}

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\left(24+220\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right.

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}{\hskip 14.22636pt}\left.+16\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert^{2}\right)C_{u_{0}}^{2}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}{+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{4}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{5}+P_{2}-6\lambda\lVert f_{ku}\rVert C_{u_{0}}^{2}\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+1}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert}.

Further, substituting the values of
P_{1},\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{4}
and
\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{5}
from (
A.10
), (
A.70
) and (
A.71
), respectively, and using the estimate (
A.28
) for
P_{2},
we write

\displaystyle\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{500}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{3}+\frac{1}{6400}\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\right)^{2}

(A.86)

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}-\Theta\sum_{j=l-2}^{r+2}\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}\rvert,

where

\displaystyle\Theta

\displaystyle:=24\lambda^{2}(C_{u_{0}})^{2}\lVert f_{uk}\rVert+38\lambda(C_{u_{0}})^{2}\lVert f_{ku}\rVert+\left(236(C_{u_{0}})^{2}+16\lambda\lVert f_{u}\rVert\right)\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert\lVert f_{uk}\rVert

\displaystyle{\hskip 14.22636pt}+\left(16\lambda^{2}C_{u_{0}}\lVert k\rVert+44\lambda^{2}(C_{u_{0}})^{2}\gamma_{2}\lVert f_{u}\rVert+8\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\lVert k\rVert+24C_{u_{0}}\right)\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert.

Finally, we obtain the desired estimate (
A.1
) since
\displaystyle\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}_{+}\right)\geq\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}\right).

A summary of the proof of Lemma
A.20
:
To derive the desired estimate for the term
\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}\right)
, we begin by introducing the modified jumps
\delta_{j}^{\prime\prime}
(see (
A.11
)) and show in Lemma
A.23
that the term
\Big\lvert\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}-\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\Big\rvert
is appropriately bounded. With this estimate in hand, we focus on the term
\mathcal{D}:=\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\right),
which we decompose as
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{Q}_{1}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}+P_{2}+\mathcal{E}_{1}.
Next, in Lemmas
A.28
and
A.30
, we reformulate the terms
\mathcal{Q}_{1}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{2}
as
\mathcal{Q}_{1}=\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}-\mathcal{E}_{4}-\mathcal{E}_{5}-\mathcal{E}_{6}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{2}=\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}+\mathcal{E}_{7}.
We then rewrite the term
\mathcal{R}_{1}
in
\mathcal{Q}_{1}
as
\mathcal{R}_{1}=\mathcal{E}_{8}+\mathcal{R}_{2}+\mathcal{R}_{3}+\mathcal{R}_{4}+\mathcal{R}_{5}
(see (
A.68
)). The above reformulations allow us to write
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}+\mathcal{R}_{4}+\mathcal{R}_{5}+P_{2}+\mathcal{E}_{9}
(see (
A.72
)), where
\mathcal{E}_{9}:=\mathcal{E}_{1}-\mathcal{E}_{4}-\mathcal{E}_{5}-\mathcal{E}_{6}+\mathcal{E}_{7}+\mathcal{E}_{8}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}:=\mathcal{Q}_{3}+\mathcal{R}_{2}+\mathcal{R}_{3}.

Now, we obtain suitable bounds for
P_{2}
(see (
A.28
)) and
\mathcal{E}_{1}
(see
A.38
). Further, through Lemmas
A.28
and
A.30
, we show that the terms
\mathcal{E}_{4},\mathcal{E}_{5},\mathcal{E}_{6}
and
\mathcal{E}_{7}
are bounded. Lemma
A.30
also provides a lower bound for
\mathcal{Q}_{1}^{*}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{*}.
In (
A.70
) and (
A.71
), we derive lower bounds for
\mathcal{R}_{4}\,\mbox{and}\,\mathcal{R}_{5},
respectively. Furthermore, in (
A.73
) we estimate the term
\mathcal{E}_{9}
, while in Lemma
A.32
, we establish a lower bound for
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{*}
. Combining these results, we derive a lower bound for
\mathcal{D}.
Using this lower bound alongside Lemma
A.23
, we finally obtain a lower bound for the term
\sum_{j=l-1}^{r+1}\left((\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})^{2}-(\delta_{j-1}^{\prime})^{2}\right),
thereby completing the proof.

A.2
Proof of Lemma
9

We present an auxiliary lemma for non-increasing sequences, which will be used in the proof of Lemma
9
.

Lemma A.35
.

Consider a non-increasing sequence
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}},
with jumps
\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}:=u_{j+1}-u_{j}.

Let
\{u^{\prime}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
be obtained from
\{u_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
by applying the time-update formula (
3.4
) and
denote the corresponding jumps
\delta_{j}^{\prime}:=u_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{\prime}-u_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{\prime}
for
{j\in\mathbb{Z}}
. Under the CFL condition (
6.1
),
the jump sequence
\{\delta_{j}^{\prime}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
satisfies the following estimate

\displaystyle\left(\delta_{j}^{\prime}\right)_{+}\leq\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right).

(A.87)

Proof A.36
.

As
\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 0
for
{j\in\mathbb{Z}},
applying the CFL condition (
6.1
) to (
A.3
), it follows that

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{8}-\kappa-\kappa^{2}\right)(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}})+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right)

(A.88)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right),

from which the estimate (
A.87
) follows directly.

Proof A.37
(Proof of Lemma
9
).

Denote the jumps
\delta_{j}^{n+1}:=u_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{n+1}-u_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n+1}
and
\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}:=u_{j}^{n}-u_{j-1}^{n}
for
{j\in\mathbb{Z}}
. The key step in deriving the estimate (
6.2
) involves decomposing
\{u_{j}^{n}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
into monotone sequences and invoking Lemmas
A.20
and
A.35
to obtain useful estimates.
To this end, we split the index set
\mathbb{Z}
of the sequence
\{u_{j}^{n}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
into maximal subsets of the form
\Gamma_{m}:=\{l-1,l,l+1,\cdots,r\},m\in S\subseteq\mathbb{N}
such that
\{u_{j}^{n}\}_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}
is either non-decreasing or non-increasing. Clearly,
\bigcup_{m\in S}\Gamma_{m}=\mathbb{Z}.

Now, for each
m\in S,
we define a new sequence
\{u^{m}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}},
which is either non-decreasing or non-increasing, as follows:

\displaystyle u^{m}_{j}

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}u_{j}^{n},\quad\,\,\,\,\,\mbox{if}\,l-1\leq j\leq r,\\
u_{l-1}^{n},\quad\mbox{if}\,\,j<l-1,\\
u_{r}^{n},\,\,\,\,\,\quad\mbox{if}\,\,j>r.\end{cases}

(A.89)

With this definition, we observe that for each
m\in S,
the jump sequence
\{\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{m}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
associated with
u^{m}_{j}
is either non-negative or non-positive. Additionally,

\displaystyle\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta^{n}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})_{+}^{2}=\sum_{m\in S}\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta^{m}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})_{+}^{2}=\sum_{m\in S}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}(\delta^{m}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}})_{+}^{2}.

(A.90)

Now, our aim is to compare the sums
\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta^{n+1}_{j})_{+}^{2}
and
\sum_{m\in S}\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta^{m^{\prime}}_{j})_{+}^{2},
where for
m\in S,

\delta^{m^{\prime}}_{j}:=u^{m^{\prime}}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}-u^{m^{\prime}}_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}},
and
\{u^{m^{\prime}}_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
is obtained by applying the scheme (
3.4
) to
\{u^{m}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}.

For this, first we note that the jumps
\{\delta_{j}^{n+1}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\delta_{j}^{n+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}+\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})-\lambda\left(f(k_{j+1},u_{j+1}^{{n+\frac{1}{2}}})-2f(k_{j},u_{j}^{{n+\frac{1}{2}}})+f(k_{j-1},u_{j-1}^{{n+\frac{1}{2}}})\right)

(A.91)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}(\sigma_{j+1}^{n}-2\sigma_{j}^{n}+\sigma_{j-1}^{n}).

Next, consider a region where the sequence
\{u_{j}^{n}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
is monotone, say for
j\in\{l-1,l,\dots,r\}.
In this case it is evident that
\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{n}=\delta_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}^{m}
for
j\in\{l-1,l,\dots,r-1\},
for some
m\in S.
Consequently, from (
A.91
) we have
\delta_{j}^{n+1}=\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}},
for
j\in\{l,l+1,\dots,r-1\}.

In other words, away from the local extremum points of
\{u_{j}^{n}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}},
the relation
\delta_{j}^{n+1}=\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}}
holds for some
m.

On the other hand, at the local extremum points, say
j\in\{l-1,r\},
of
\{u_{j}^{n}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
with
\{u_{j}^{n}\}
non-decreasing on
j\in\{l-1,l,\dots,r\},
we have

\displaystyle\delta_{r}^{n+1}

\displaystyle=\delta_{r}^{m^{\prime}}+\delta_{r}^{(m+1)^{\prime}}+\lambda f_{k}(\bar{k}_{r+\frac{1}{2}},u_{r}^{n})\Delta k_{r+\frac{1}{2}}-\lambda f_{k}(\bar{k}_{r-\frac{1}{2}},u_{r}^{n})\Delta k_{r-\frac{1}{2}},

(A.92)

\displaystyle\delta_{l-1}^{n+1}

\displaystyle=\delta_{l-1}^{(m-1)^{\prime}}+\delta_{l-1}^{m^{\prime}}+\lambda f_{k}(\bar{k}_{l-\frac{1}{2}},u_{l-1}^{n})\Delta k_{l-\frac{1}{2}}-\lambda f_{k}(\bar{k}_{l-\frac{3}{2}},u_{l-1}^{n})\Delta k_{l-\frac{3}{2}},

for
\bar{k}_{r-\frac{1}{2}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{r-1},k_{r}),

\bar{k}_{r+\frac{1}{2}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{r},k_{r+1}),

\bar{k}_{l-\frac{1}{2}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{l-1},k_{l})
and
\bar{k}_{l-\frac{3}{2}}\in\mathcal{I}(k_{l-2},\\
k_{l-1}).

Further, using Lemma
A.35
for the term
(\delta_{r}^{(m+1)^{\prime}})_{+}
(which is generated from the non-increasing sequence
\{u^{m+1}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}
) in (
A.92
), along with the property that
a,b\in\mathbb{R},

(a+b)_{+}\leq a_{+}+b_{+},
we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\delta_{r}^{n+1}\right)_{+}

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{r}^{m^{\prime}})_{+}+(\delta_{r}^{(m+1)^{\prime}})_{+}+\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert(\lvert\Delta k_{r+\frac{1}{2}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{r-\frac{1}{2}}\rvert)

(A.93)

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{r}^{m^{\prime}})_{+}+2\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert(\lvert\Delta k_{r+\frac{1}{2}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{r-\frac{1}{2}}\rvert).

Upon squaring both sides of (
A.93
) and using the bound
\lvert\delta_{r}^{m^{\prime}}\rvert\leq 2C_{u_{0}}
(a consequence of Theorem
8
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\left(\delta_{r}^{n+1}\right)_{+}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{r}^{m^{\prime}})_{+}^{2}+\tilde{\Psi}\left(\lvert\Delta k_{r+\frac{1}{2}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{r-\frac{1}{2}}\rvert\right),

(A.94)

where
\tilde{\Psi}:=16\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{k}\rVert^{2}\lVert k\rVert+8C_{u_{0}}\lambda\lVert f_{k}\rVert.

Analogous arguments for
j=l-1
yield

\displaystyle\left(\delta_{l-1}^{n+1}\right)_{+}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(\delta_{l-1}^{m^{\prime}})_{+}^{2}+\tilde{\Psi}\left(\lvert\Delta k_{l-\frac{1}{2}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{l-\frac{3}{2}}\rvert\right).

(A.95)

Thus, we observe that for any
{j\in\mathbb{Z}},
either
(\delta_{j}^{n+1})_{+}^{2}=(\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}})_{+}^{2}
or
(\delta_{j}^{n+1})_{+}^{2}\leq(\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}})_{+}^{2}+\tilde{\Psi}(\lvert\Delta k_{j+\frac{1}{2}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{j-\frac{1}{2}}\rvert),
for some
m\in S.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}\left(\delta_{j}^{n+1}\right)_{+}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m\in S}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}\left(\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}}\right)_{+}^{2}+2\tilde{\Psi}\lVert k\rVert_{BV}

(A.96)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\in S_{\uparrow}}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}\left(\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}}\right)_{+}^{2}+\sum_{m\in S_{\downarrow}}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}\left(\delta_{j}^{m^{\prime}}\right)_{+}^{2}+2\tilde{\Psi}\lVert k\rVert_{BV}

where
S_{\uparrow}:=\{m\in S:\{u^{m}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}\,\mbox{is a non-decreasing sequence}\}
and
S_{\downarrow}:=\{m\in S:\{u^{m}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}\mbox{is a non-increasing sequence}\}.
Finally, invoking Lemma
A.20
for
\{u^{m}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}},\,m\in S_{\uparrow}
and Lemma
A.35
for
\{u^{m}_{j}\}_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}},\,m\in S_{\downarrow}
in (
A.96
) and recollecting (
A.90
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}\left(\delta_{j}^{n+1}\right)_{+}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m\in S_{\uparrow}}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{m})_{+}^{2}-\frac{1}{500}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{m\in S_{\uparrow}}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{m})_{+}^{3}

(A.97)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6400}\sum_{m\in S_{\uparrow}}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}\left(\Delta^{2}\delta_{{j-\frac{3}{2}}}^{m}\right)^{2}+3\Theta\lVert k\rVert_{BV}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\sum_{m\in S_{\downarrow}}\sum_{j\in\Gamma_{m}}\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{k}\rVert^{2}\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right)^{2}+2\tilde{\Psi}\lVert k\rVert_{BV}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})_{+}^{2}-\frac{1}{500}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})_{+}^{3}+3\Theta\lVert k\rVert_{BV}+8\lVert k\rVert\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{k}\rVert^{2}\lVert k\rVert_{BV}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+2\tilde{\Psi}\lVert k\rVert_{BV}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})_{+}^{2}-\frac{1}{500}\lambda\gamma_{1}\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}(\delta_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}^{n})_{+}^{3}+\Psi\lVert k\rVert_{BV},

where
\Psi:=3\Theta+8\lVert k\rVert\lambda^{2}\lVert f_{k}\rVert^{2}+2\tilde{\Psi}.

Here, we have used the fact that

\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right)^{2}\leq 4\lVert k\rVert\sum_{{j\in\mathbb{Z}}}\left(\lvert\Delta k_{{j+\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert+\lvert\Delta k_{{j-\frac{1}{2}}}\rvert\right)\leq 8\lVert k\rVert\lVert k\rVert_{BV}.

This completes the proof.
